 succeeded in 193ms:
     1	"""OFFLINE-ONLY CLI: sign a validated RulePack payload with a real Ed25519 key.
     2	
     3	Spec §1 module layout (``research/visa/2026-07-17-visa-oracle-v2-round2-
     4	codex-engine-concretization.md``): ``scripts/visa_engine/sign_pack.py`` —
     5	"offline only; private key never present in FastAPI".
     6	
     7	**This script NEVER runs inside the FastAPI process.** It exists solely for
     8	the offline signing ceremony an operator runs by hand, on their own machine,
     9	against a key file that never leaves local disk. ``bundle.py``'s own module
    10	docstring states the FIREBREAK this script is the signing half of: "key
    11	generation/registration is the operator's ceremony, never autonomous code"
    12	— nothing in this package imports ``sign_pack`` from any server code path,
    13	and it must never be added to one.
    14	
    15	Builds the exact spec §3 signing envelope (verbatim from ``bundle.py``'s
    16	module docstring, which this script's signing-input construction
    17	independently re-derives rather than importing — see ``_SIGNING_DOMAIN``
    18	below for why that duplication is deliberate)::
    19	
    20	    signed_bytes =
    21	        UTF8("balizero.visa-rulepack.v1")
    22	        || 0x00
    23	        || JCS(protected)
    24	        || 0x00
    25	        || JCS(payload)
    26	
    27	Refuses to sign unless the payload FIRST passes ``compile_pack.
    28	compile_pack_source``'s gate, re-run internally on every invocation — a
    29	signature over a broken pack is worse than no signature: it carries the full
    30	weight of a "trusted" bundle while still failing every downstream evaluator
    31	invariant.
    32	
    33	SECURITY RAILS (absolute):
    34	
    35	* Never prints/logs the private key's raw bytes or anything derived from it
    36	  that could reconstruct it. The derived PUBLIC key IS printed on success —
    37	  that is not a secret, it is the exact value an operator pins into a
    38	  ``StaticTrustStore``.
    39	* Loads the PEM in-process only, via ``cryptography``'s own PEM parser —
    40	  never shells out to ``openssl`` or any external process that could leak
    41	  key material via argv, a shell history file, or a subprocess log.
    42	* Refuses to sign if the key file is not ``chmod 0600`` (or stricter) — the
    43	  mode is checked via ``fstat`` on the SAME file descriptor the content is
    44	  then read from (never a separate stat-by-path followed by a separate
    45	  open-by-path), so there is no TOCTOU window for a symlink swap between
    46	  the check and the read. No byte of the file's CONTENT is read before the
    47	  mode check passes.
    48	* Refuses ``--environment PRODUCTION`` unless ``--i-know-this-is-production``
    49	  is EXACTLY ``True`` (strict identity check, not truthiness — a caller
    50	  passing the string ``"false"`` must not silently bypass the gate).
    51	* Refuses an ``--output`` that would overwrite ``--key-file`` or the
    52	  payload source, and writes the signed bundle atomically (temp file +
    53	  rename) so a crash mid-write can never leave a truncated ``--output``.
    54	
    55	Usage::
    56	
    57	    PYTHONPATH=. python -m backend.scripts.visa_engine.sign_pack \\
    58	        backend/services/visa_engine/contracts/packs/rulepack-test-c1-tourism.source.json \\
    59	        --kid key-2026-07-test-1 \\
    60	        --key-file /path/to/2026-07-test-1.ed25519.pem \\
    61	        --environment TEST \\
    62	        --sequence 1 \\
    63	        --output backend/services/visa_engine/contracts/packs/rulepack-test-c1-tourism.signed.json
    64	"""
    65	
    66	from __future__ import annotations
    67	
    68	import argparse
    69	import base64
    70	import hashlib
    71	import json
    72	import os
    73	import re
    74	import stat
    75	import sys
    76	import tempfile
    77	from datetime import datetime, timezone
    78	from pathlib import Path
    79	
    80	from cryptography.exceptions import UnsupportedAlgorithm
    81	from cryptography.hazmat.primitives import serialization
    82	from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric.ed25519 import Ed25519PrivateKey
    83	from pydantic import ValidationError
    84	
    85	from backend.scripts.visa_engine.compile_pack import compile_pack_source, format_report
    86	from backend.services.visa_engine.bundle import (
    87	    StaticTrustStore,
    88	    TrustedSigningKey,
    89	    canonicalize_json,
    90	)
    91	from backend.services.visa_engine.bundle import verify_rule_pack as bundle_verify_rule_pack
    92	from backend.services.visa_engine.enums import Environment
    93	from backend.services.visa_engine.errors import RulePackVerificationError
    94	from backend.services.visa_engine.models import IDENTIFIER_PATTERN, utc_isoformat
    95	
    96	#: Independently re-derived (NOT imported from ``bundle.py``'s private
    97	#: ``_SIGNING_DOMAIN``) as a deliberate cross-check on the production
    98	#: signing-input formula — the same convention already established by
    99	#: ``backend/tests/services/visa_engine/_builders.py``'s
   100	#: ``sign_rule_pack_envelope`` (see that function's docstring for the
   101	#: identical rationale: "doubles as a cross-check on the production
   102	#: signing-input formula" rather than importing the private constant).
   103	_SIGNING_DOMAIN = b"balizero.visa-rulepack.v1"
   104	
   105	#: Group/other permission bits — a key file's mode must have NONE of these
   106	#: set (0600 or stricter, e.g. 0400).
   107	_KEY_FILE_FORBIDDEN_MODE_BITS = 0o077
   108	
   109	#: A PKCS8 Ed25519 PEM private key is ~120 bytes. 1 MiB is wildly generous
   110	#: headroom (encrypted/PKCS12-wrapped variants, comments, stray whitespace)
   111	#: while still bounding the read loop against an attacker who replaces the
   112	#: key path with a very large regular file (PR-A1 codex round-2 finding #2).
   113	_MAX_KEY_FILE_BYTES = 1 << 20
   114	
   115	_KID_PATTERN = re.compile(IDENTIFIER_PATTERN)
   341	def sign_pack(
   342	    *,
   343	    payload_source: Path,
   344	    kid: str,
   345	    key_file: Path,
   346	    environment: str,
   347	    sequence: int,
   348	    output: Path,
   349	    i_know_this_is_production: bool = False,
   350	    signed_at: datetime | None = None,
   351	) -> tuple[dict, str]:
   352	    """Run the full offline signing ceremony and return
   353	    ``(signed_envelope_dict, public_key_b64url)``.
   354	
   355	    Raises ``SignPackError`` on any refusal. Never partially signs, never
   356	    writes ``output`` itself (the caller — ``main`` — owns that write, only
   357	    after this function returns successfully) so a refusal never leaves a
   358	    half-written file behind.
   359	
   360	    Order of checks (cheapest / least-sensitive-resource-touching first):
   361	    (1) ``--environment PRODUCTION`` gate, (2) ``--output`` never collides
   362	    with ``--key-file``/``payload_source``, (3) the compile_pack gate
   363	    (re-validates + statically compiles the payload), (4) environment/
   364	    sequence cross-checks against the payload's own fields, (5) ``--kid``
   365	    shape, (6) payload.created_at is not after signed_at (a pack may not claim
   366	    to have been created after the signature proving its bytes existed),
   367	    (7) the private key file (permission check, then load) — the sensitive
   368	    resource is touched last, only once everything else already checks out.
   369	    (8) sign. (9) self-verify via the REAL
   370	    ``bundle.verify_rule_pack`` before ever returning.
   371	    """
   372	
   373	    # Strict identity check (PR-A1 codex correctness review finding #3), mirroring bundle.py's
   374	    # own `allow_unsigned is not True` pattern (see that module's
   375	    # `_verify_unsigned_dev` docstring): `i_know_this_is_production` is
   376	    # typed as `bool`, but Python does not enforce that at runtime — a
   377	    # caller passing the non-empty STRING `"false"` is truthy, and
   378	    # `not "false"` is `False`, silently DISABLING this gate. Only the
   379	    # exact value `True` satisfies it.
   380	    if environment == "PRODUCTION" and i_know_this_is_production is not True:
   381	        raise SignPackError(
   382	            "refusing to sign for --environment PRODUCTION without --i-know-this-is-production"
   383	        )
   384	
   385	    # PR-A1 codex correctness review finding #1: catch an --output that would clobber the
   386	    # key file or the payload source BEFORE any I/O that could act on it —
   387	    # cheap path-string work, no reason to defer it past the PRODUCTION gate.
   388	    _check_distinct_paths(payload_source=payload_source, key_file=key_file, output=output)
   389	
   390	    try:
   391	        payload, report = compile_pack_source(payload_source)
   392	    except OSError as exc:
   393	        raise SignPackError(f"cannot read {payload_source}: {exc}") from exc
   394	    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
   395	        raise SignPackError(f"{payload_source} is not valid JSON: {exc}") from exc
   396	    except ValidationError as exc:
   397	        raise SignPackError(
   398	            f"{payload_source} does not validate as a RulePackPayload:\n{exc}"
   399	        ) from exc
   400	
   401	    if not report.ok:
   402	        raise SignPackError(
   403	            "refusing to sign — compile_pack gate failed:\n" + format_report(report)
   404	        )
   405	
   406	    if payload.environment.value != environment:
   407	        raise SignPackError(
   408	            f"--environment {environment!r} does not match the payload's own "
   409	            f"environment {payload.environment.value!r} — refusing to sign a pack "
   410	            "for a different environment than the one asserted on the command line"
   411	        )
   412	    if payload.sequence != sequence:
   413	        raise SignPackError(
   414	            f"--sequence {sequence} does not match the payload's own sequence "
   415	            f"{payload.sequence} — refusing to sign; sequence is the anti-rollback "
   416	            "chain's own field, a mismatch here is almost always the wrong file"
   417	        )
   418	
   419	    if not _KID_PATTERN.fullmatch(kid):
   420	        raise SignPackError(
   421	            f"--kid {kid!r} does not match the Identifier pattern "
   422	            f"{IDENTIFIER_PATTERN!r} (must start with a letter) — "
   423	            "ProtectedHeader.kid would fail model validation downstream"
   424	        )
   425	
   426	    # BEFORE the key file is opened: the module's own ordering doctrine says the
   427	    # sensitive resource is touched last, once everything else already checks
   428	    # out. A payload this guard will refuse must not cause a private key to be
   429	    # read — so `signed_at_dt` is resolved here too; it needs no key.
   430	    signed_at_dt = signed_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
   431	    assert_created_before_signed(payload.created_at, signed_at_dt)
   432	
   433	    private_key = _load_private_key(key_file)
   434	
   435	    protected: dict = {
   436	        "domain": "balizero.visa-rulepack.v1",
   437	        "alg": "Ed25519",
   438	        "kid": kid,
   439	        "signed_at": utc_isoformat(signed_at_dt),
   440	        "schema_version": "1.0.0",
   441	        "environment": environment,
   442	    }
   443	    payload_dict = payload.model_dump(mode="json", by_alias=True)
   444	
   445	    protected_bytes = canonicalize_json(protected)
   446	    payload_bytes = canonicalize_json(payload_dict)
   447	    payload_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(payload_bytes).hexdigest()
   448	
   449	    signed_bytes = _SIGNING_DOMAIN + b"\x00" + protected_bytes + b"\x00" + payload_bytes
   450	    signature = private_key.sign(signed_bytes)
   451	    signature_b64url = base64.urlsafe_b64encode(signature).rstrip(b"=").decode("ascii")
   452	
   453	    envelope: dict = {
   454	        "canonicalization": "RFC8785",
   455	        "protected": protected,
   340	        key_id=kid,
   341	        public_key=public_key,
   342	        valid_from=_parse_utc_datetime(valid_from_raw),
   343	        valid_to=_parse_utc_datetime(valid_to_raw) if valid_to_raw is not None else None,
   344	        revoked_at=_parse_utc_datetime(revoked_at_raw) if revoked_at_raw is not None else None,
   345	        environment=environment,
   346	    )
   347	
   348	
   349	# --- Canonicalization ---------------------------------------------------------
   350	
   351	
   352	def canonicalize_json(value: Mapping[str, JsonValue]) -> bytes:
   353	    """RFC 8785 JSON Canonicalization Scheme (JCS) serialization of ``value``.
   354	
   355	    Thin pass-through to the ``rfc8785`` library (Trail of Bits, spec §3) —
   356	    deliberately NOT ``json.dumps(sort_keys=True)``, which does not
   357	    normalize number formatting or property ordering per the ECMAScript
   358	    ``JSON.stringify`` rules JCS requires. Any ``rfc8785.CanonicalizationError``
   359	    (NaN/Infinity, lone surrogates, integers outside the safe JSON-number
   360	    range) propagates unwrapped from THIS function — it stays a generic,
   361	    honest JCS canonicalizer usable outside the trust boundary too (e.g. the
   362	    test-only signing fixture in ``_builders.sign_rule_pack_envelope``,
   363	    where a ``RulePackVerificationError`` would be the wrong error to see
   364	    while *producing* a signature).
   365	
   366	    Trust-boundary callers do NOT call this directly: ``verify_rule_pack``/
   367	    ``_verify_unsigned_dev`` route every wire-object canonicalization
   368	    through the private ``_canonicalize_wire_object`` wrapper below, which
   369	    is what actually translates a ``CanonicalizationError`` into
   370	    :class:`~backend.services.visa_engine.errors.RulePackVerificationError`
   371	    — a wire string that is syntactically valid JSON (passes JSON Schema's
   372	    unadorned ``type: string``, e.g. a lone UTF-16 surrogate) but that RFC
   373	    8785 cannot serialize must never escape ``verify_rule_pack`` as a bare
   374	    third-party exception.
   375	    """
   376	
   377	    return rfc8785.dumps(value)
   378	
   379	
   380	def _canonicalize_wire_object(value: Mapping[str, JsonValue], *, label: str) -> bytes:
   381	    """Canonicalize a signed-envelope wire sub-object (``"protected"`` or
   382	    ``"payload"``) for the trust boundary, translating any RFC 8785
   383	    canonicalization failure into the module's typed error contract.
   384	    ``label`` names the sub-object in the raised message (``"protected"``/
   385	    ``"payload"``) for diagnosability."""
   386	
   387	    try:
   388	        return canonicalize_json(value)
   389	    except rfc8785.CanonicalizationError as exc:
   390	        raise RulePackVerificationError(f"{label} is not JCS-canonicalizable: {exc}") from exc
   391	
   392	
   393	def _build_signed_bytes(protected_bytes: bytes, payload_bytes: bytes) -> bytes:
   394	    """Exact signing input per spec §3::
   395	
   396	    signed_bytes =
   397	        UTF8("balizero.visa-rulepack.v1")
   398	        || 0x00
   399	        || JCS(protected)
   400	        || 0x00
   401	        || JCS(payload)
   402	    """
   403	
   404	    return _SIGNING_DOMAIN + b"\x00" + protected_bytes + b"\x00" + payload_bytes
   405	
   406	
   407	def _decode_base64url_no_padding(value: str) -> bytes:
   408	    """Base64url-decode ``value``, which must carry NO padding characters —
   409	    a correctly unpadded base64url string never contains ``=``. Used for
   410	    both the RulePack ``signature`` field and trust-store public keys.
   680	        raise RulePackVerificationError(
   681	            "rule pack envelope 'protected'/'payload' must be JSON objects"
   682	        )
   683	
   684	    # Belt-and-suspenders: the compiler's own header/environment check
   685	    # re-enforces this too, but the cryptographic trust boundary should not
   686	    # depend on a downstream compile step ever running.
   687	    protected_environment = raw_protected.get("environment")
   688	    payload_environment = raw_payload.get("environment")
   689	    if protected_environment != payload_environment:
   690	        raise RulePackVerificationError(
   691	            f"protected header environment {protected_environment!r} does not "
   692	            f"match payload environment {payload_environment!r}"
   693	        )
   694	
   695	    protected_bytes = _canonicalize_wire_object(raw_protected, label="protected")
   696	    payload_bytes = _canonicalize_wire_object(raw_payload, label="payload")
   697	
   698	    computed_payload_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(payload_bytes).digest()
   699	    declared_payload_sha256 = envelope.get("payload_sha256")
   700	    if computed_payload_sha256.hex() != declared_payload_sha256:
   701	        raise RulePackVerificationError(
   702	            f"payload_sha256 mismatch: envelope declares {declared_payload_sha256!r}, "
   703	            f"computed {computed_payload_sha256.hex()!r} from SHA256(JCS(payload)) "
   704	            "— the payload was tampered with, or the declared hash is stale"
   705	        )
   706	
   707	    raw_kid = raw_protected.get("kid")
   708	    raw_signed_at = raw_protected.get("signed_at")
   709	    signed_at = _parse_utc_datetime(raw_signed_at)
   710	    signing_key = trust_store.resolve(
   711	        key_id=raw_kid,
   712	        signed_at=signed_at,
   713	        environment=protected_environment,
   714	    )
   715	
   716	    # Future-skew guard (3-seat verify FIX-NOW #3): `observed_at` was
   717	    # accepted as a parameter but never actually used on the signed path —
   718	    # a pack signed_at any arbitrary point in the future relative to
   719	    # observed_at sailed through untouched (a partial backdating/
   720	    # future-dating defense gap). Checked AFTER trust_store.resolve()
   721	    # succeeds so a key already invalid/expired/revoked/not-yet-valid at
   722	    # signed_at is still reported as exactly that, not shadowed by this
   723	    # check. NOT a fix for revocation-at-activation-time against a
   724	    # compromised+backdated key — that needs an authoritative clock plus
   725	    # persistence (current_sequence/current_payload_sha256 lookups), which
   726	    # is `validate_activation`'s caller's job in a later PR; this is only
   727	    # the partial, stateless mitigation available at verify-on-load time.
   728	    if signed_at > observed_at + _SIGNED_AT_FUTURE_TOLERANCE:
   729	        raise RulePackVerificationError(
   730	            f"signed_at {signed_at.isoformat()!r} is in the future relative "
   731	            f"to observed_at {observed_at.isoformat()!r} beyond the "
   732	            f"{_SIGNED_AT_FUTURE_TOLERANCE} tolerance"
   733	        )
   734	
   735	    raw_signature = envelope.get("signature")
   736	    signature_bytes = _decode_signature(raw_signature)
   737	    signed_bytes = _build_signed_bytes(protected_bytes, payload_bytes)
   738	
   739	    try:
   740	        signing_key.public_key.verify(signature_bytes, signed_bytes)
   741	    except InvalidSignature as exc:
   742	        raise RulePackVerificationError(
   743	            f"Ed25519 signature verification failed for key {raw_kid!r}"
   744	        ) from exc
   745
